Use after adding or modifying definitions, or when the user asks to test, try out, or verify their MCP surface. - **field-test**: Nova 방법론을 실제 프로젝트에서 검증해 개선 포인트를 찾을 때. — MUST TRIGGER: 신규 Nova 기능 검증, 사용자가 실전 피드백을 요청할 때, 워크트리 격리 환경에서 방법론을 리허설할 때. ([TeamSPWK/nova](https://github.com/TeamSPWK/nova/tree/HEAD/dev/skills/field-test) · ★2)
- **field-test**: Unattended QA pass over one repo: captures every surface at several widths, with touch emulation, on Slow 3G (web) and with Dynamic Type, dark mode and a throttled/offline backend (iOS), judges what is broken or feels off, fixes it on a local branch with one commit per fix, saves a before and after PNG per fix and writes report.md incrementally. Never asks questions, never pushes, never touches the main checkout. NOT for a full screenshot catalog (/screens) and NOT for pre-push review (/audit). Use when the user runs /field-test or wants a responsive, touch and slow-network test with fixes. ([raaaf/claude-skills](https://github.com/raaaf/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/field-test))
- **field-test**: Run a code change live and prove the behaviour it touches, either on a local rig of stubs or on the user's production qui server, then clean up. Covers backend changes through the API and frontend changes through a real browser (headless Playwright on a rig, Claude in Chrome on production, service-worker traps, screenshots). The unit is one device-observed defect (or one device QA pass over a flow).
